), yields

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{\left\langle\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle}}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\operatorname{D}^{\sharp}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}+\left\langle\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\operatorname{D}^{\sharp}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}+\left\langle\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}],\boldsymbol{R}_{1}\boldsymbol{R}_{2}^{T}+\boldsymbol{R}_{1}^{T}\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\sharp}_{t}(\boldsymbol{R}\boldsymbol{P})

\displaystyle=(\operatorname{D}^{\sharp}_{t}\boldsymbol{R})\boldsymbol{P}+\boldsymbol{R}(\operatorname{D}^{\sharp}_{t}\boldsymbol{P})+\boldsymbol{R}\left(\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\right)\boldsymbol{P}

for all
\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2},\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
and
\boldsymbol{P}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
where
\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]
is vanishing if and only if the material carries out a rigid body motion.

For extended 2-tensor fields
\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}_{h}
, which are sufficing
\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}|_{\xi=0}=\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
we conclude from (
9
) and (
55
) that that for the upper-convected
\mathbb{R}^{3}
-time derivative

\displaystyle\dot{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}-(\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}})\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}(\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}})^{T}

\displaystyle\rightarrow\operatorname{D}^{\sharp}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}

is valid for
h\rightarrow 0
.

2.4
Lower-Convected Derivative

In order to obtain the lower-convected derivative, we choose a pullback for the time derivative (
2
),
which adhere to the covariant material proxy instead of the contravariant material proxy as it is stipulated for the upper-convected derivative.
We give the exact definition for the vector and tensor field case in its associated subsections.
In contrast to
[
6
]
, we use the short naming “lower-convected”
for “lower-lower-convected” or “fully-lower-convected”,
since we do not treat any mixed-convected derivative in this paper.

2.4.1
Vector Fields

We consider the lower-convected pullback

\Phi^{*_{\flat}}_{t,\tau}:\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S},t+\tau}\rightarrow\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S},t}

given by

\displaystyle(\Phi^{*_{\flat}}_{t,\tau}\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]|_{t+\tau})(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}):=r_{i}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\partial^{i}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})+\phi[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})

for decompositions (
10
) of vector fields
\boldsymbol{R}
,
where
\partial^{i}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}:=g_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{ij}\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}
at all
(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})
.
With this we define the upper-convected derivative by
\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}:=\operatorname{D}_{t}|_{\Phi^{*}_{t,\tau}=\Phi^{*_{\flat}}_{t,\tau}}
,
i. e. the time derivative (
2
) yields

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]

\displaystyle=g_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{ij}(\partial_{t}r_{j}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}])\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}+(\partial_{t}\phi[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}])\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]

w. r. t. the material observer locally at material events
(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})
.
With (
51
), this is relatable to the upper-convected derivative (
17
) by

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]

\displaystyle=\operatorname{D}^{\sharp}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+(\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}},\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}},\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}])\boldsymbol{r}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\,\text{.}

This expression can be represented by an observer-invariant formulation.
Therefore, we omit the observer argument in square brackets.
Moreover, since all normal parts of
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]
(
18
) are antisymmetric,
i. e. it is
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]=\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]
valid,
we conclude the following corollary.

Corollary 8
.

For all
\boldsymbol{R}=\boldsymbol{r}+\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
\boldsymbol{r}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
and
\phi\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
holds

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}+\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\boldsymbol{R}=\mathfrak{L}^{\flat}\boldsymbol{r}+\dot{\phi}\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{.}

(22)

The tangential lower-convected derivative
\mathfrak{L}^{\flat}
on tangential vector fields is given
in table
4
.
Note that in contrast to the material or Jaumann derivative, the lower-convected derivative is not compatible with the inner product in general.
Substituting (
22
) into (
12
) yields

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{\left\langle\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle}}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}+\left\langle\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}-\left\langle\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}],\boldsymbol{R}_{1}\otimes\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

for all
\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
where
\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]
is vanishing if and only if the material carries out a rigid body motion.

For extended vector fields
\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}_{h}
, which are sufficing
\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}|_{\xi=0}=\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
we conclude from (
9
) and (
55
) that for the lower-convected
\mathbb{R}^{3}
-time derivative

\displaystyle\dot{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}+(\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}})^{T}\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}

\displaystyle\rightarrow\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}

is valid for
h\rightarrow 0
.

2.4.2
2-Tensor Fields

We consider the lower-convected pullback

\Phi^{*_{\flat}}_{t,\tau}:\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}^{2}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S},t+\tau}\rightarrow\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}^{2}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S},t}

given by

\displaystyle(\Phi^{*_{\flat}}_{t,\tau}\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]|_{t+\tau})(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})

\displaystyle=r_{ij}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\partial^{i}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\otimes\partial^{j}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\eta_{Li}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\partial^{i}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\eta_{Rj}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\otimes\partial^{j}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\phi[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})

for decompositions (
13
) of 2-tensor fields
\boldsymbol{R}
.
With this we define the upper-convected derivative by
\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}:=\operatorname{D}_{t}|_{\Phi^{*}_{t,\tau}=\Phi^{*_{\flat}}_{t,\tau}}
,
i. e. the time derivative (
2
) yields

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]

\displaystyle=(\partial_{t}r_{ij}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}])g_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{ik}g_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{jl}\partial_{k}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}\otimes\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}+(\partial_{t}\phi[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}])\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+(\partial_{t}\eta_{Li}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}])g_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{ik}\partial_{k}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+(\partial_{t}\eta_{Rj}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}])g_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{jl}\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\otimes\partial_{l}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}

w. r. t. the material observer locally at material events
(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})
.
With (
52
) and (
51
), this is relatable to the upper-convected derivative (
20
) by

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]

\displaystyle=\operatorname{D}^{\sharp}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+2\boldsymbol{S}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}},\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\boldsymbol{r}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+2\boldsymbol{r}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\boldsymbol{S}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}},\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+2\boldsymbol{S}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}},\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+2\boldsymbol{\nu}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\otimes\boldsymbol{S}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}},\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\,\text{,}

where
2\boldsymbol{S}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}},\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]:=\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}},\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}},\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]=\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]
.
This expression can be represented by an observer-invariant formulation.
Therefore, we omit the observer argument in square brackets and conclude the following corollary.

Corollary 9
.

For all
\boldsymbol{R}=\boldsymbol{r}+\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}+\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,

\boldsymbol{r}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L},\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
and
\phi\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
holds

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}+\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\boldsymbol{R}+\boldsymbol{R}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]=\mathfrak{L}^{\flat\flat}\boldsymbol{r}+\mathfrak{L}^{\flat}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\mathfrak{L}^{\flat}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}+\dot{\phi}\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{.}

(23)

Tangential lower-convected derivatives
\mathfrak{L}^{\flat\flat}
and
\mathfrak{L}^{\flat}
on tangential tensor fields are given
in table
6
and
4
.
Note that in contrast to the material or Jaumann derivative, the upper-convected derivative is neither compatible with the inner nor the tensor product in general.
Substituting (
23
) into (
15
),
resp. (
23
) and (
22
) into (
16
), yields

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{\left\langle\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle}}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}+\left\langle\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}-\left\langle\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}],\boldsymbol{R}_{1}\boldsymbol{R}_{2}^{T}+\boldsymbol{R}_{1}^{T}\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}(\boldsymbol{R}\boldsymbol{P})

\displaystyle=(\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R})\boldsymbol{P}+\boldsymbol{R}(\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{P})-\boldsymbol{R}\left(\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\right)\boldsymbol{P}

for all
\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2},\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
and
\boldsymbol{P}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
where
\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]
is vanishing if and only if the material carries out a rigid body motion.

For extended 2-tensor fields
\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}_{h}
, which are sufficing
\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}|_{\xi=0}=\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
we conclude from (
9
) and (
55
) that for the lower-convected
\mathbb{R}^{3}
-time derivative

\displaystyle\dot{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}+(\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}})^{T}\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}+\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}(\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}})

\displaystyle\rightarrow\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}

is valid for
h\rightarrow 0
.

2.5
Jaumann Derivative

For the sake of simplicity, we define the Jaumann derivative by

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}:=\frac{1}{2}\left(\operatorname{D}^{\sharp}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}+\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}\right)

(24)

for all
\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{n}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{n}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{n}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{n}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.94333pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.94115pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10081pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
instead stipulating a pullback
\Phi^{*_{\mathcal{J}}}_{t,\tau}
, see the discussion in section
2.5.3
for more details.
Therefore, the Jaumann derivative is observer-invariant a priori, since the upper- and lower-convected are observer-invariant.
Note that the Jaumann derivative is also often named corotational derivative.

2.5.1
Vector Fields

By defining the skew-symmetric tensor field

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\mathcal{A}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]

\displaystyle:=\frac{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]-\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]}{2}=\frac{\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}-(\nabla\boldsymbol{v}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}})^{T}}{2}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\left(\nabla v_{\bot}+\boldsymbol{I\!I}\boldsymbol{v}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}\right)-\left(\nabla v_{\bot}+\boldsymbol{I\!I}\boldsymbol{v}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}\right)\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}\,\text{,}

(25)

we deduce the following corollary by (
24
), (
19
) and (
22
).

Corollary 10
.

For all
\boldsymbol{R}=\boldsymbol{r}+\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
\boldsymbol{r}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
and
\phi\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
holds

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}-\boldsymbol{\mathcal{A}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\boldsymbol{R}=\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{r}+\dot{\phi}\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{.}

(26)

The tangential Jaumann derivative
\mathfrak{J}
on tangential vector fields is given
in table
4
.
Since
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{A}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]
is skew-symmetric, substituting (
26
) into (
12
) yields the following corollary.

Corollary 11
.

The Jaumann derivative on vector fields is compatible with the inner product,
i. e. for all
\boldsymbol{R}_{1}=\boldsymbol{r}_{1}+\phi_{1}\boldsymbol{\nu},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}=\boldsymbol{r}_{2}+\phi_{2}\boldsymbol{\nu}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
holds

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{\left\langle\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle}}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}+\left\langle\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{r}_{1},\boldsymbol{r}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}+\left\langle\boldsymbol{r}_{1},\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{r}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}+\dot{\phi}_{1}\phi_{2}+\phi_{1}\dot{\phi}_{2}\,\text{.}

For extended vector fields
\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}_{h}
, which are sufficing
\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}|_{\xi=0}=\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
we conclude from (
9
) and (
55
) that for the Jaumann
\mathbb{R}^{3}
-time derivative

\displaystyle\dot{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}-\left(\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}-(\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}})^{T}\right)\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}

\displaystyle\rightarrow\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}

is valid for
h\rightarrow 0
.

2.5.2
2-Tensor Fields

Using the tensor field
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{A}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]
(
25
),
we deduce the following corollary by (
24
), (
21
) and (
23
).

Corollary 12
.

For all
\boldsymbol{R}=\boldsymbol{r}+\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}+\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,

\boldsymbol{r}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L},\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
and
\phi\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
holds

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}-\boldsymbol{\mathcal{A}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\boldsymbol{R}+\boldsymbol{R}\boldsymbol{\mathcal{A}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]=\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{r}+\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}+\dot{\phi}\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{.}

(27)

The tangential Jaumann derivative
\mathfrak{J}
is given
in table
6
for tangential 2-tensor fields and in table
4
for tangential vector fields.
Since
\boldsymbol{\mathcal{A}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]
is skew-symmetric, substituting (
27
) into (
15
) yields the following corollary.

Corollary 13
.

The Jaumann derivative on 2-tensor fields is compatible with the inner product,
i. e. for all
\boldsymbol{R}_{\alpha}=\boldsymbol{r}_{\alpha}+\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\alpha L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}_{\alpha R}+\phi_{\alpha}\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
with
\alpha=1,2
, holds

\displaystyle\dot{\overline{\left\langle\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle}}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}+\left\langle\boldsymbol{R}_{1},\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{r}_{1},\boldsymbol{r}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}+\left\langle\boldsymbol{r}_{1},\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{r}_{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}+\dot{\phi}_{1}\phi_{2}+\phi_{1}\dot{\phi}_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{1L},\boldsymbol{\eta}_{2L}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}+\left\langle\boldsymbol{\eta}_{1L},\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{2L}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}+\left\langle\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{1R},\boldsymbol{\eta}_{2R}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}+\left\langle\boldsymbol{\eta}_{1R},\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{2R}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}\,\text{.}

Substituting (
27
) and (
26
) into (
16
) results in the following corollary.

Corollary 14
.

The Jaumann derivative is compatible with the 2-tensor-vector product,
i. e. for all
\boldsymbol{R}=\boldsymbol{r}+\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}+\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}

and
\boldsymbol{P}=\boldsymbol{p}+\psi\boldsymbol{\nu}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
,

\boldsymbol{r}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L},\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R},\boldsymbol{p}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
and
\phi,\psi\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
holds

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}(\boldsymbol{R}\boldsymbol{P})

\displaystyle=(\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R})\boldsymbol{P}+\boldsymbol{R}(\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{P})

(28)

\displaystyle=(\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{r})\boldsymbol{p}+\boldsymbol{r}(\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{p})+\psi\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}+\dot{\psi}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}+\left(\dot{\phi}\psi+\phi\dot{\psi}+\left\langle\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R},\boldsymbol{p}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}+\left\langle\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R},\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{p}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}\right)\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{.}

For extended 2-tensor fields
\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}_{h}
, which are sufficing
\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}|_{\xi=0}=\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
we conclude from (
9
) and (
55
) that for the Jaumann
\mathbb{R}^{3}
-time derivative

\displaystyle\dot{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}-\left(\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}-(\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}})^{T}\right)\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}+\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}\left(\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}-(\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}})^{T}\right)

\displaystyle\rightarrow\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{R}

is valid for
h\rightarrow 0
.

2.5.3
Discussion of Approach (
24
)

The choice of a pullback in (
2
) is indeed sufficient to determine the associated time derivative,
but it is not necessary, i. e. we can find other pullbacks, which define the same time derivative.
The fully Taylor expansion

\displaystyle(\Phi^{*}_{t,\tau}\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]|_{t+\tau})(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})=\sum_{\alpha=0}^{\infty}\frac{\tau^{\alpha}}{\alpha!}(\operatorname{D}^{(\alpha)}_{t}\boldsymbol{R})[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})

at
\tau=0
might be the easiest way to see this, where
\operatorname{D}^{(\alpha)}_{t}
is a time derivative of
\alpha
th order.
The reason we mention this is that we stipulate the identity (
24
) to define the Jaumann derivative in relation to the upper- and lower-convected derivative.
Indeed, averaging the associated pullbacks to
\Phi^{*_{\mathcal{J}}}_{t,\tau}:=\frac{1}{2}\left(\Phi^{*_{\sharp}}_{t,\tau}+\Phi^{*_{\flat}}_{t,\tau}\right)
in the same way
would be sufficient to obtain the Jaumann derivative (
24
) also by
\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}=\operatorname{D}_{t}|_{\Phi^{*}_{t,\tau}=\Phi^{*_{\mathcal{J}}}_{t,\tau}}
.
However, despite this proceeding would be reasoned, it is not very intuitive.
More tangible would be a pullback structurally given by

\Phi^{*_{\mathcal{J}}}_{t,\tau}\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]|_{t+\tau}:=\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{t,\tau}^{T}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]|_{t+\tau}
for vector fields,
resp.
\Phi^{*_{\mathcal{J}}}_{t,\tau}\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]|_{t+\tau}:=\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{t,\tau}^{T}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]|_{t+\tau}+\boldsymbol{R}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]|_{t+\tau}\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{t,\tau}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]

for 2-tensor fields,
where
\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{t,\tau}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
is the rotation tensor, which rotate every local tangential plane and normal according to the material deformation

\mathcal{S}|_{t}\rightarrow\mathcal{S}|_{t+\tau}
.
It is only ensured that this pullback equals the former pullback first orderly w. r. t. the Taylor expansion above.
For the sake of simplicity we decided to approach the Jaumann derivative by (
24
) rather than determining
\boldsymbol{\Omega}_{t,\tau}^{-1}
and its necessary derivatives.

2.6
Q-Tensor Fields

2.6.1
General Q-Tensor Fields

Beside the orthogonal decomposition

\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}=\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}\oplus(\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})\oplus(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S})\oplus(\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})

realized by (
13
), there is another useful orthogonal decomposition for 2-tensor fields, namely

\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}=(\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}\boldsymbol{Id})\oplus\mathchoice{\operatorname{A}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{A}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displayst ,
where
\boldsymbol{Id}
is the Euclidean identity tensor fields, e. g. implemented by
\boldsymbol{Id}=\delta^{AB}\boldsymbol{e}_{A}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{B}
w. r. t. a Cartesian frame,

\mathchoice{\operatorname{A}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{A}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{A}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{A}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}:=\{\boldsymbol{A}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}:\boldsymbol{A}=-\boldsymbol{A}^{T}\}
is the space of skew-symmetric tensor fields and

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\boldsymbol{Q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}:\boldsymbol{Q}=\boldsymbol{Q}^{T}\text{ and }\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{Q}=0\right\}

is the space of symmetric and trace-free tensor fields, also called Q-tensor fields.
In this section we examine the latter in context of time derivatives in more detail.

To describe
\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
, which established a 5-dimensional vector bundle on
\mathcal{S}
, by tangential quantities,
we introduce the orthogonal decomposition

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Q}}\left[\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{\eta},\beta\right]:=\boldsymbol{q}+\boldsymbol{\eta}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}+\beta\left(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right)\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}\,\text{,}

(29)

where
\boldsymbol{q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}:=\{\boldsymbol{q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}:\boldsymbol{q}=\boldsymbol{q}^{T}\text{ and }\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{q}=0\}
is a tangential Q-tensor field,
and
\boldsymbol{\eta}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
and
\beta=\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
are determined uniquely for all
\boldsymbol{Q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
.

\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}
is the tangential identity tensor fields, e. g. implemented by
\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}=g^{ij}\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{X}\otimes\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X}
w. r. t. the local tangential frame
or
\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}=(\delta^{AB}-\nu^{A}\otimes\nu^{B})\boldsymbol{e}_{A}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{B}
w. r. t. a Cartesian frame.
This decomposition is consistent to decomposition (
13
) for
\boldsymbol{R}=\boldsymbol{Q}
,
\boldsymbol{r}=\boldsymbol{q}-\frac{\beta}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}
,

\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}=\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}=\boldsymbol{\eta}
and
\phi=\beta
.
Therefore, (
3
), (
27
),
\dot{\boldsymbol{q}}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
and
\dot{\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}}=\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}=0
yields

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Q}}\left[\dot{\boldsymbol{q}}-2\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{\eta}\otimes\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]),\dot{\boldsymbol{\eta}}+\boldsymbol{q}\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]-\frac{3\beta}{2}\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}],\dot{\beta}+2\left\langle\boldsymbol{\eta},\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}\right]\,\text{,}

(30)

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Q}}\left[\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{q},\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{\eta},\dot{\beta}\right]\,\text{,}

(31)

where
