\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}:\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}\rightarrow\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
is the orthogonal projection given by
\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}\boldsymbol{r}=\frac{1}{2}(\boldsymbol{r}+\boldsymbol{r}^{T}-(\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{r})\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}})
for all

\boldsymbol{r}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
.
As a consequence, the space of Q-tensor fields is closed by the material derivative as well as the Jaumann derivative.
Unfortunately, the upper- and lower-convected derivative fail this behavior.
Symmetric tensor fields are closed by them, but trace-free tensor fields are not, since (
21
) and (
23
) yield

\displaystyle\operatorname{Tr}\operatorname{D}^{\sharp}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}=-\operatorname{Tr}\operatorname{D}^{\flat}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=-2\left\langle\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}],\boldsymbol{Q}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}=\beta\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]-2\left\langle\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}],\boldsymbol{q}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}\,\text{,}

which is only vanishing for rigid body motions in general.

Note that for an eigenvector field
\boldsymbol{P}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
with eigenvalue field
\lambda\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
i. e. it holds
\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{P}=\lambda\boldsymbol{P}
, yields

\displaystyle(\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q})\boldsymbol{P}

\displaystyle=\dot{\lambda}\boldsymbol{P}-(\boldsymbol{Q}-\lambda\boldsymbol{Id})\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{P}\,\text{,}

\displaystyle(\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q})\boldsymbol{P}

\displaystyle=\dot{\lambda}\boldsymbol{P}-(\boldsymbol{Q}-\lambda\boldsymbol{Id})\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{P}

by (
16
) and (
28
).
Since
\boldsymbol{Q}
is a real-valued symmetric 2-tensor field, the union of all eigenspaces is spanning
\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
.
As a consequence we obtain the following corollary.

Corollary 15
.

For all
\boldsymbol{Q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
\boldsymbol{P}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
and
\lambda\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
s. t.
\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{P}=\lambda\boldsymbol{P}
and
\dot{\lambda}=0
is valid, holds

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{P}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=0\,\text{,}

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{P}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=0\,\text{.}

The converses are not true without further ado.
The main reason is that the eigenvector fields of a Q-tensor field do not have to be differentiable, neither spatially nor temporally.
If the Q-tensor field comprises
\pm\frac{1}{2}
-defects, eigenvector fields even has to be discontinuous to represent such defects.
Certainly, it is feasible to show the converses by modifying the time-derivatives w. r. t. sign-sensitivity for instance.
However, for the sake simplicity, we leave this issue as an open question in this paper.
Note that corollary
15
would not hold in the same way for the upper- and lower convected derivative.

2.6.2
Surface Conforming Q-Tensor Fields

One useful subset of the space of Q-tensor fields is the space of surface conforming Q-tensor fields

\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}:=\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Q}}[\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S},0,\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}]
, which is a subtensor field of the Q-tensor field space, i. e.
\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}<\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}<\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
, see
[
14
,
5
,
15
]
.
The associated orthogonal projection
\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}:\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}\rightarrow\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
is given by

\displaystyle\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle:=\boldsymbol{Q}-\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{\nu})\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{\nu})

(32)

for all
\boldsymbol{Q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
.
Since decomposition (
29
) yields
\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{\nu}=\boldsymbol{\eta}+\beta\boldsymbol{\nu}
,
we could summarize the situation in the following corollary.

Corollary 16
.

A Q-tensor field
\boldsymbol{Q}=\boldsymbol{q}+\boldsymbol{\eta}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}+\beta\left(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right)\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}

with
\boldsymbol{q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
\boldsymbol{\eta}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
and
\beta=\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
, is surface conforming,
if and only if one of the following equivalent statements is true:

(i)

\boldsymbol{Q}=\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}\boldsymbol{Q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,

(ii)

\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{v})=\boldsymbol{\eta}=0
,

(iii)

\boldsymbol{\nu}
is an eigenvector field of
\boldsymbol{Q}
and
\beta
is its associated eigenvalue.

In contrast to the Jaumann derivative (
31
),
the space of surface conforming Q-tensor fields is not closed by the material derivative (
30
),
since
\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}((\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q})\boldsymbol{v})=\boldsymbol{q}\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]-\frac{3\beta}{2}\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]
is not vanishing generally for
\boldsymbol{Q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
.
To obtain such a closing we use the orthogonal projection (
32
) and call the resulting time derivative

\operatorname{D}^{\operatorname{C}_{\mathcal{S}},{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}:=\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}\circ\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}|_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}
surface conforming material derivative.
Taken all together, this yields

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\operatorname{C}_{\mathcal{S}},{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Q}}[\dot{\boldsymbol{q}},0,\dot{\beta}]

\displaystyle=\dot{\boldsymbol{q}}+\dot{\beta}\left(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right)

\displaystyle\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}

(33)

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Q}}[\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{q},0,\dot{\beta}]

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{q}+\dot{\beta}\left(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right)

\displaystyle\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}

(34)

for all surface conforming Q-tensor fields
\boldsymbol{Q}=\boldsymbol{q}+\beta(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}})\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
where
\boldsymbol{q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
and
\beta\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
.

One simple special case of conforming Q-tensor fields are the tangential Q-tensor fields in
\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}=\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Q}}[\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S},0,0]<\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
.
Here,
\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
is closed by the surface conforming material derivative (
33
), resp. Jaumann derivative (
34
),
which coincides with the tangential material derivative, resp. tangential Jaumann derivative, given in
[
6
]
.

2.6.3
Surface Landau-de Gennes models

As already demonstrated in
[
6
,
7
]
for tangential tensor-fields the dynamics of these models differ. To sensitize the reader for this difference in applying the models, e.g. in the context of morphogenesis
[
8
,
9
,
10
]
, is the main motivation for this research.

As a simple, but not trivial, example we consider the one-constant Landau-de Gennes free energy

\displaystyle\mathfrak{U}[\boldsymbol{Q}]

\displaystyle:=\frac{L}{2}\left\|\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{Q}\right\|_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{3}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{3}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{3}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{3}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}^{2}+\int_{\mathcal{S}}a\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{Q}^{2}+\frac{2b}{3}\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{Q}^{3}+c\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{Q}^{4}\operatorname{d}\mathcal{S}

(35)

for Q-tensor fields
\boldsymbol{Q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
, elastic parameter
L>0
and thermotropic coefficients
a,b,c\in\mathbb{R}
.
Moreover, we assume that the surface
\mathcal{S}
is boundaryless, i. e.
\partial\mathcal{S}=\emptyset
,
and the motion of the surface is prescribed by the material velocity
\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
.
The associated
\operatorname{L}^{\!2}
-gradient flow with dynamics driven by

\operatorname{D}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}\in\{\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q},\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}\}
is

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}\mathfrak{U}=L\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{Q}-2\left(a\boldsymbol{Q}+b\left(\boldsymbol{Q}^{2}-\frac{\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{Q}^{2})}{3}\boldsymbol{Id}\right)+c\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{Q}^{2})\boldsymbol{Q}\right)\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}\,\text{,}

(36)

where the
\operatorname{L}^{\!2}
-gradient
\nabla_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}\mathfrak{U}
is given by
variation of the energy in arbitrary directions of
\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
, i. e.
\left\langle\nabla_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}\mathfrak{U},\boldsymbol{R}\right\rangle_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}:=\left\langle\frac{\delta\mathfrak{U}}{\delta\boldsymbol{Q}},\boldsymbol{R}\right\rangle_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}

with aid of lemma
25
, which justifies the surface Laplace operator.
Note that
\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
is closed by
\operatorname{D}_{t}=\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}
as well as
\operatorname{D}_{t}=\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}
in
\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
, see (
30
) and (
31
),
i. e. one could safely use
\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}
and
\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}
given in table
6
.
For a pure tangential motion of the surface, i. e.
v_{\bot}=0
, and a therefore valid Eulerian observer, i. e.
\boldsymbol{V}=0
, equation (
36
)
equals the Q-tensor equation of the surface Beris-Edwards model in
[
15
]
for the Jaumann derivative.
Note that
\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{Q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
holds already, therefore we do not need to apply an extra projection into the space of Q-tensor fields.
This can immediately be deduced from lemma
24
.

The situation changes if we like to consider the Landau-de Gennes energy (
35
) w. r. t. surface conforming Q-tensor fields
\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
.
The associated gradient flow can be obtained either by variation and weak testing in the right space,
i. e. using

\left\langle\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}\operatorname{D}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q},\boldsymbol{R}\right\rangle_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}=-\left\langle\frac{\delta\mathfrak{U}}{\delta\boldsymbol{Q}},\boldsymbol{R}\right\rangle_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}
for all
\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
or by Lagrange multiplier technique.
Both approaches lead to the same result as we see below.
Since
\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{\nu}=\beta\boldsymbol{\nu}
holds for all
\boldsymbol{Q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
according to corollary
16
,
we infer
\boldsymbol{Q}^{2}\boldsymbol{\nu}=\beta^{2}\boldsymbol{\nu}
and from that in turn

\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}\left((\boldsymbol{Q}^{2}-\frac{\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{Q}^{2})}{3}\boldsymbol{Id})\boldsymbol{\nu}\right)=0
.
Or in other words, if
\boldsymbol{Q}
is conforming, then so is the Q-tensor part of
\boldsymbol{Q}^{2}
.
This leads to the
\operatorname{L}^{\!2}
-gradient flow

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}^{\operatorname{C}_{\mathcal{S}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=-\nabla_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}\mathfrak{U}=L\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}^{\operatorname{C}_{\mathcal{S}}}\boldsymbol{Q}-2\left(a\boldsymbol{Q}+b\left(\boldsymbol{Q}^{2}-\frac{\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{Q}^{2})}{3}\boldsymbol{Id}\right)+c\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{Q}^{2})\boldsymbol{Q}\right)\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}

(37)

for
\boldsymbol{Q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
, where
\operatorname{D}^{C}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}
is one of the time derivatives in
\{\operatorname{D}^{\operatorname{C}_{\mathcal{S}},{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q},\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}\}
,

\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}^{\operatorname{C}_{\mathcal{S}}}:=\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}\circ\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}|_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}
is the surface conforming Laplace operator
and
\left\langle\nabla_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}\mathfrak{U},\boldsymbol{R}\right\rangle_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}:=\left\langle\frac{\delta\mathfrak{U}}{\delta\boldsymbol{Q}},\boldsymbol{R}\right\rangle_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoic defines the surface conforming
\operatorname{L}^{\!2}
-gradient for all
\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
.
Alternatively, adding the Lagrange function

\mathcal{C}[\boldsymbol{Q},\boldsymbol{\lambda}]:=\left\langle\boldsymbol{\lambda},\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{v})\right\rangle_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S})}
,
where
