is the Lagrange multiplier,
to the Landau-de Gennes energy (
35
) yields

\displaystyle\operatorname{D}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=L\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{Q}-2\left(a\boldsymbol{Q}+b\left(\boldsymbol{Q}^{2}-\frac{\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{Q}^{2})}{3}\boldsymbol{Id}\right)+c\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{Q}^{2})\boldsymbol{Q}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left(\boldsymbol{\lambda}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\lambda}\right)\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}\,\text{,}

(38)

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{Q}\boldsymbol{v})\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}\,\text{,}

(39)

where
\operatorname{D}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}\in\{\operatorname{D}^{\,{\!\mathfrak{m}}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q},\operatorname{D}^{\mathcal{J}}_{t}\boldsymbol{Q}\}
.
Substituting (
39
) into (
38
) and applying
\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}
on both sides of (
38
) also results in
the surface conforming
\operatorname{L}^{\!2}
-gradient flow (
37
).
In contrast to the
\operatorname{L}^{\!2}
-gradient flow (
36
), the effort to rephrase the conforming flow (
37
)
according to decomposition (
29
) is significantly less.
For tangential time derivatives
\operatorname{d}_{t}\boldsymbol{q}\in\{\dot{\boldsymbol{q}},\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{q}\}
given in table
6
,
we obtain the system of tangential Q-tensor and scalar equations

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \operatorname{d}_{t}\boldsymbol{q}&=L\left(\Delta\boldsymbol{q}-\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})\boldsymbol{q}+3\beta\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})\right)-\left(2a-2b\beta+3c\beta^{2}+2c\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{q}^{2}\right)\boldsymbol{q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}\,\text{,}\\
\dot{\beta}&=L\left(\Delta\beta+\left\langle\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2},2\boldsymbol{q}-3\beta\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}\right)-\left(2a+b\beta+3c\beta^{2}+2c\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{q}^{2}\right)\beta+\frac{2}{3}b\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{q}^{2}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}\,\text{,}\end{aligned}

(40)

which are equivalent to (
37
), see
B.2
for a detailed derivation.
We could substitute
\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}=\mathcal{H}\boldsymbol{I\!I}-\mathcal{K}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}
for the third fundamental form, where
\mathcal{K}:=\det\{I\!I^{i}_{j}\}
is the Gaussian curvature.
For the material derivative this yields the surface Landau-de Gennes model in
[
14
]
up to the uniaxiality constrain used there.
It gives also the same tangential Q-tensor equation in
[
5
]
for a constant
\beta
. Implications of the choice of the time derivative
\operatorname{d}_{t}\boldsymbol{q}\in\{\dot{\boldsymbol{q}},\mathfrak{J}\boldsymbol{q}\}
in these models needs to be explored numerically.

Appendix A
Identities

Lemma 17
.

For a parameterization
\boldsymbol{X}
holds

\displaystyle\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X}

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{ij}^{k}\partial_{k}\boldsymbol{X}+I\!I_{ij}\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{,}

(41)

\displaystyle\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{\nu}

\displaystyle=-I\!I^{j}_{i}\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X}

(42)

\displaystyle\text{resp.\ }\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X},\partial_{k}\boldsymbol{X}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{ijk}\,\text{,}

(43)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X},\boldsymbol{\nu}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=-\left\langle\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{\nu},\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}=I\!I_{ij}\,\text{.}

(44)

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{\nu},\boldsymbol{\nu}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=0

(45)

Proof.

Equation (
43
) is an alternative definition of the Christoffel symbols if the metric tensor is given by

g_{ij}=\left\langle\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{X},\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}
.
Equations (
44
) are equivalent definitions of the second fundamental form, since (
45
) is true,
which in turn holds by
\left\|\boldsymbol{\nu}\right\|_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}=1
.
Identities (
41
) and (
42
) summarize (
43
), (
44
) and (
45
).
∎

Lemma 18
.

For the metric tensor holds

\displaystyle\partial_{l}g_{ij}

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{lij}+\Gamma_{lji}

(46)

\displaystyle\partial_{l}g^{ij}

\displaystyle=-\left(g^{kj}\Gamma_{lk}^{i}+g^{ki}\Gamma_{lk}^{j}\right)\,\text{.}

(47)

Proof.

Both identities are consequences of the metric compatibility, i. e.
0=g_{ij|l}=\partial_{l}g_{ij}-\Gamma_{li}^{k}g_{kj}-\Gamma_{lj}^{k}g_{ik}
and

0=\mathchoice{g^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.76544pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.76544pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.91959pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|l}}}}{g^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.76544pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.76544pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.91959pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|l}}}}{g^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.76544pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.76544pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.91959pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|l}}}}{g^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.76544pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.76544pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.91959pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.0854pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|l}}}}=\partial_{l}g^{ij}+\Gamma_{lk}^{i}g^{kj}+\Gamma_{lk}^{j}g^{ik}
.
Alternatively, (
46
) is a consequence of (
43
),
and (
47
) of (
46
) by evaluating

\partial_{l}g^{ij}=\partial_{l}(g^{ik}g^{jm}g_{km})
with the aid of the product rule.
∎

Lemma 19
.

For a time-depending parameterization
\boldsymbol{X}
with velocity
\boldsymbol{V}=\boldsymbol{v}+v_{\bot}\boldsymbol{\nu}=\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{X}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
holds

\displaystyle\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{V}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{V}]^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{V}]^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{V}]^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{V}]^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}\boldsymbol{e}_{A}=\mathchoice{G^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{G^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{G^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{G^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}[\boldsymbol{V}]\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X}+b_{i}[\boldsymbol{V}]\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{,}

(48)

where

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}]

\displaystyle:=\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}(\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{V})=\nabla\boldsymbol{v}-v_{\bot}\boldsymbol{I\!I}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}

(49)

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}]

\displaystyle:=\boldsymbol{\nu}\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{V}=\nabla v_{\bot}+\boldsymbol{I\!I}\boldsymbol{v}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}\,\text{.}

(50)

Proof.

See
[
7
]
.
∎

Lemma 20
.

For time-dependent covariante proxy components
r_{ij}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
\eta_{i}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
contravariant proxy components
r^{ij}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
and
\eta^{i}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}

of a tangential 2-tensor fields
\boldsymbol{r}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
and vector field
\boldsymbol{\eta}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
holds

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\eta_{i}

\displaystyle=g_{ik}\partial_{t}\eta^{k}+\left[(\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}])\boldsymbol{\eta}\right]_{i}\,\text{,}

(51)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}r_{ij}

\displaystyle=g_{ik}g_{jl}\partial_{t}r^{kl}+\left[\boldsymbol{r}(\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}])+(\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}]+\boldsymbol{G}^{T}[\boldsymbol{V}])\boldsymbol{r}\right]_{ij}\,\text{,}

(52)

with
\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}]\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
given in (
49
).

Proof.

Follows by
\partial_{t}g_{ij}=G_{ij}[\boldsymbol{V}]+G_{ji}[\boldsymbol{V}]
(see
[
6
]
),

r_{ij}=g_{ik}g_{jl}r^{kl}
,
\eta_{i}=g_{ik}\eta^{k}
and product rule.
∎

Lemma 21
.

For a time-depending parameterization
\boldsymbol{X}
with velocity
\boldsymbol{V}=\boldsymbol{v}+v_{\bot}\boldsymbol{\nu}=\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{X}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
holds

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\nu}=\partial_{t}(\nu^{A}\boldsymbol{e}_{A})

\displaystyle=-\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}]\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}\,\text{,}

(53)

\displaystyle\text{resp.\ }\partial_{t}\nu^{A}

\displaystyle=-b^{i}[\boldsymbol{V}]\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{X}^{A}\,\text{,}

where
\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}]
is given in (
50
).

Proof.

Follows from

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\nu},\boldsymbol{\nu}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}\left\langle\boldsymbol{\nu},\boldsymbol{\nu}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}-\left\langle\boldsymbol{\nu},\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\nu}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}=-\left\langle\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\nu},\boldsymbol{\nu}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}\overset{\Rightarrow}{=}0

and

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\nu},\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{X}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}\left\langle\boldsymbol{\nu},\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{X}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}-\left\langle\boldsymbol{\nu},\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{V}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}\overset{\eqref{eq:partialder_V}}{=}-b_{i}[\boldsymbol{V}]\,\text{.}

∎

Corollary 22
.

For a time-depending parameterization
\boldsymbol{X}
with velocity
\boldsymbol{V}=\boldsymbol{v}+v_{\bot}\boldsymbol{\nu}=\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{X}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
and
a tangential vector field
\boldsymbol{u}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
holds

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\nu}+u^{k}\partial_{k}\boldsymbol{\nu}=\partial_{t}(\nu^{A}\boldsymbol{e}_{A}+u^{k}\partial_{k}\nu^{A}\boldsymbol{e}_{A})

\displaystyle=-\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}+\boldsymbol{u}]\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}\,\text{,}

(54)

\displaystyle\text{resp.\ }\partial_{t}\nu^{A}+u^{k}\partial_{k}\nu^{A}

\displaystyle=-b^{i}[\boldsymbol{V}+\boldsymbol{u}]\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{X}^{A}\,\text{,}

where
\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}+\boldsymbol{u}]=\nabla v_{\bot}+\boldsymbol{I\!I}(\boldsymbol{v}+\boldsymbol{u})
is consistent with definition (
50
).

Proof.

Follows from (
53
) and (
42
).
∎

Lemma 23
.

For a time-depending parameterization
\boldsymbol{X}
, with velocity
\boldsymbol{V}=\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{X}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
thin film parameterization
\boldsymbol{\chi}[\boldsymbol{X}]
and thin film velocity
\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}=\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}[\boldsymbol{X}]\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}_{h}=\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}_{h}}

holds

\displaystyle\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}\rightarrow\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}[\boldsymbol{V}]=\boldsymbol{G}[\boldsymbol{V}]+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}]-\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}]\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}

(55)

for
h\rightarrow 0
.

Proof.

In the following, we omit the argument
\boldsymbol{X}
in square brackets.
The thin film parameterization (
6
) yields the frame

\displaystyle\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{\chi}

\displaystyle=\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{X}-\xi I\!I_{i}^{j}\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X}\,\text{,}

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi}\boldsymbol{\chi}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{.}

(56)

Regarding this frame, the covariant thin film proxy of the velocity

\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}=\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{\chi}=\boldsymbol{V}-\xi\boldsymbol{b}[\boldsymbol{V}]
is given by

\displaystyle\widehat{V}_{i}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}},\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{\chi}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}_{h}}=v_{i}-\xi\left(b_{i}[\boldsymbol{V}]+I\!I_{ij}v^{j}\right)+\mathcal{O}(\xi^{2})\,\text{,}

\displaystyle\widehat{V}_{\xi}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}},\partial_{\xi}\boldsymbol{\chi}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}_{h}}=v_{\bot}\,\text{.}

The Christoffel symbols of second kind w. r. t. the thin film frame (
56
) are

\displaystyle\text{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\mathbb{L}$}}}_{ij}^{k}=\Gamma_{ij}^{k}+\mathcal{O}(\xi)\,\text{,}\quad\text{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\mathbb{L}$}}}_{ij}^{\xi}=I\!I_{ij}+\mathcal{O}(\xi)\,\text{,}\quad\text{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\mathbb{L}$}}}_{\xi\xi}^{K}=\text{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\mathbb{L}$}}}_{I\xi}^{\xi}=\text{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\mathbb{L}$}}}_{\xi I}^{\xi}=0\,\text{,}\quad\text{and}\quad\text{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\mathbb{L}$}}}_{i\xi}^{k}=\text{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\mathbb{L}$}}}_{\xi i}^{k}

\displaystyle=-I\!I_{i}^{k}+\mathcal{O}(\xi)\,\text{,}

where a capital Latin letter
I,J,K
comprises a small Latin letter
i,j,k
and
\xi
, see
[
13
]
for more details.
Therefore the covariant thin film proxy of the velocity gradient

\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}=\delta_{B}^{C}\partial_{C}\widehat{V}^{A}\boldsymbol{e}_{A}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{B}
yields

\displaystyle[\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}]_{ij}

\displaystyle=\partial_{j}\widehat{V}_{i}-\text{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\mathbb{L}$}}}_{ij}^{K}\widehat{V}_{K}=\partial_{j}v_{i}-\Gamma_{ij}^{k}v_{k}-v_{\bot}I\!I_{ij}+\mathcal{O}(\xi)

\displaystyle=G_{ij}[\boldsymbol{V}]+\mathcal{O}(\xi)

\displaystyle[\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}]_{i\xi}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\xi}\widehat{V}_{i}-\text{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\mathbb{L}$}}}_{\xi i}^{K}\widehat{V}_{K}=-b_{i}[\boldsymbol{V}]-I\!I_{ij}v^{j}+I\!I_{i}^{k}v_{k}+\mathcal{O}(\xi)

\displaystyle=-b_{i}[\boldsymbol{V}]+\mathcal{O}(\xi)

\displaystyle[\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}]_{\xi j}

\displaystyle=\partial_{j}\widehat{V}_{\xi}-\text{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\mathbb{L}$}}}_{j\xi}^{K}\widehat{V}_{K}=\partial_{j}v_{\bot}+I\!I_{j}^{k}v_{k}+\mathcal{O}(\xi)

\displaystyle=b_{j}[\boldsymbol{V}]+\mathcal{O}(\xi)

\displaystyle[\operatorname{\widehat{\nabla}}\widehat{\boldsymbol{V}}]_{\xi\xi}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\xi}\widehat{V}_{\xi}-\text{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\mathbb{L}$}}}_{\xi\xi}^{K}\widehat{V}_{K}

\displaystyle=0\,\text{.}

The orthogonality
\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{\chi}\bot\partial_{\xi}\boldsymbol{\chi}

and the thin film limit of the covariant tangential proxy of the thin film metric tensor w. r. t. frame (
56
), which is
g_{ij}
, see
[
13
]
,
implies (
55
) finally.
∎

Lemma 24
.

The surface Laplace operator
\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}:\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}\rightarrow\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
equals the Cartesian-componentwise Laplace-Beltrami operator,
i. e. for all
\boldsymbol{R}=R^{AB}\boldsymbol{e}_{A}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{B}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
holds

\displaystyle\left[\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{R}\right]^{AB}

\displaystyle=\Delta R^{AB}=g^{ij}\left(\partial_{i}\partial_{j}R^{AB}-\Gamma_{ij}^{k}\partial_{k}R^{AB}\right)\,\text{.}

Proof.

Applying product rule yields

\displaystyle\left[(\operatorname{Tr}\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}^{2})\boldsymbol{R}\right]^{AB}

\displaystyle=\delta_{CD}g^{kl}\partial_{l}\left(g^{ij}\partial_{j}R^{AB}\partial_{i}X^{C}\right)\partial_{k}X^{D}=g^{jl}\partial_{l}\partial_{j}R^{AB}+g^{kl}g^{ij}\left\langle\partial_{l}\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{X},\partial_{k}\boldsymbol{X}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}\partial_{j}R^{AB}+(\partial_{i}g^{ij})\partial_{j}R^{AB}\,\text{.}

Substituting
g^{kl}\left\langle\partial_{l}\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{X},\partial_{k}\boldsymbol{X}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}=\Gamma_{li}^{l}
(
43
)
and
\partial_{i}g^{ij}=-(g^{jk}\Gamma_{ik}^{i}+g^{ik}\Gamma_{ik}^{j})
(
47
)
gives the assertion.
∎

Lemma 25
.

The surface Laplace operator
\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}:\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}\rightarrow\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
corresponds to the Bochner-like Laplace operator
given by the surface derivative
\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}
, i. e. for all
\boldsymbol{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
holds

\displaystyle\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{R}=-\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}^{*}\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{R}\,\text{.}

Proof.

Neglecting any boundary terms, lemma
24
yields

\displaystyle\left\langle\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{R},\boldsymbol{\Psi}\right\rangle_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\Delta R^{AB},\Psi_{AB}\right\rangle_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}=-\left\langle\nabla R^{AB},\nabla\Psi_{AB}\right\rangle_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{1}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{1}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{1}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{1}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}=-\left\langle\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{R},\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{\Psi}\right\rangle_{\operatorname{L}^{\!2}(\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{3}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{3}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{3}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{3}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}})}

for all
\boldsymbol{R},\boldsymbol{\Psi}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
.
∎

Corollary 26
.

For all
\boldsymbol{R}=\boldsymbol{r}+\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}+\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,

\boldsymbol{r}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L},\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}{\operatorname{T}\!}\mathcal{S}
and
\phi\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
the surface Laplace operator
\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}:\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}\rightarrow\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
yields

\displaystyle\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{R}

\displaystyle=\Delta\boldsymbol{r}-\left(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\boldsymbol{r}+\boldsymbol{r}\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\right)-2\left((\nabla\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L})\boldsymbol{I\!I}+\boldsymbol{I\!I}(\nabla\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R})^{T}\right)-\left(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\nabla\mathcal{H}+\nabla\mathcal{H}\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}\right)+2\phi\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(2(\nabla\boldsymbol{r}):\boldsymbol{I\!I}+\boldsymbol{r}\nabla\mathcal{H}+\Delta\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}-\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}-\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\left(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}+2\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}\right)-2\boldsymbol{I\!I}\nabla\phi-\phi\nabla\mathcal{H}\right)\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}

\displaystyle\quad+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\left(2(\nabla\boldsymbol{r}^{T}):\boldsymbol{I\!I}+(\nabla\mathcal{H})\boldsymbol{r}+\Delta\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}-\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}-\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\left(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}+2\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\right)-2\boldsymbol{I\!I}\nabla\phi-\phi\nabla\mathcal{H}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(2\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}:\boldsymbol{r}+2(\nabla\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}+\nabla\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}):\boldsymbol{I\!I}+(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}+\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R})\nabla\mathcal{H}+\Delta\phi-2\phi\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})\right)\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{.}

(57)

Proof.

In this proof we calculate
\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{R}=\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{r}+\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})+\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R})+\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}(\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})

term by term in this order using
\left[\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{R}\right]^{AB}=g^{ij}\left(\partial_{j}\partial_{i}R^{AB}-\Gamma_{ij}^{k}\partial_{k}R^{AB}\right)
(lemma
24
).
This is a straightforward proceeding, where we mainly use
\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X}=\Gamma_{ij}^{k}\partial_{k}\boldsymbol{X}+I\!I_{ij}\boldsymbol{\nu}
(
41
)
and
\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{\nu}=-I\!I^{j}_{i}\partial_{j}\boldsymbol{X}
(
42
), without mentioning it every time.
Mixed proxy components
\mathchoice{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{r}]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{r}]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{r}]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{r}]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}=\partial_{k}r^{AB}
yield

\displaystyle\partial_{k}r^{AB}

\displaystyle=\partial_{k}\left(r^{ij}\partial_{i}X^{A}\partial_{j}X^{B}\right)=\partial_{k}r^{ij}\partial_{i}X^{A}\partial_{j}X^{B}+r^{ij}\left(\Gamma_{ki}^{l}\partial_{l}X^{A}\partial_{j}X^{B}+\Gamma_{kj}^{l}\partial_{i}X^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}+I\!I_{ki}\nu^{A}\partial_{j}X^{B}+I\!I_{kj}\partial_{i}X^{A}\nu^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{r^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.06631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.9045pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|k}}}}{r^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.06631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.9045pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|k}}}}{r^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.06631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.9045pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|k}}}}{r^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.06631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.9045pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|k}}}}\partial_{i}X^{A}\partial_{j}X^{B}+r^{ij}\left(I\!I_{ki}\nu^{A}\partial_{j}X^{B}+I\!I_{kj}\partial_{i}X^{A}\nu^{B}\right)\,\text{.}

Substituting this into
\left[\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{r}\right]^{AB}
, the product rule gives the summands

\displaystyle g^{ij}\partial_{j}\left(\mathchoice{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.02527pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.02527pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.04922pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17801pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|i}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.02527pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.02527pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.04922pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17801pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|i}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.02527pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.02527pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.04922pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17801pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|i}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.02527pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.02527pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.04922pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.17801pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|i}}}}\partial_{l}X^{A}\partial_{m}X^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\mathchoice{r^{{{lm|j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.3961pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.3961pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.52019pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.80011pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{r^{{{lm|j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.3961pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.3961pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.52019pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.80011pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{r^{{{lm|j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.3961pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.3961pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.52019pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.80011pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{r^{{{lm|j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[19.3961pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[19.3961pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[9.52019pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[6.80011pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}+g^{ij}\Gamma_{ji}^{k}\mathchoice{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.06631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.9045pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|k}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\m
