2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}\left(I\!I^{j}_{l}\nu^{A}\partial_{m}X^{B}+I\!I^{j}_{m}\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}\right)

\displaystyle g^{ij}\partial_{j}\left(r^{lm}I\!I_{il}\nu^{A}\partial_{m}X^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}I\!I^{j}_{l}\nu^{A}\partial_{m}X^{B}+r^{lm}\left(I\!I^{j}_{l|j}+g^{ij}\Gamma_{ji}^{k}I\!I_{kl}\right)\nu^{A}\partial_{m}X^{B}-r^{lm}I\!I_{l}^{j}I\!I^{k}_{j}\partial_{k}X^{A}\partial_{m}X^{B}+r^{lm}I\!I_{l}^{j}I\!I_{jm}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}

\displaystyle g^{ij}\partial_{j}\left(r^{lm}I\!I_{im}\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}I\!I^{j}_{m}\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}+r^{lm}\left(I\!I^{j}_{m|j}+g^{ij}\Gamma_{ji}^{k}I\!I_{km}\right)\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}-r^{lm}I\!I_{m}^{j}I\!I^{k}_{j}\partial_{l}X^{A}\partial_{k}X^{B}+r^{lm}I\!I_{m}^{j}I\!I_{jl}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}

\displaystyle-g^{ij}\Gamma_{ij}^{k}\partial_{k}r^{AB}

\displaystyle=-g^{ij}\Gamma_{ij}^{k}\left(\mathchoice{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.06631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.9045pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|k}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.06631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.9045pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|k}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.06631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.9045pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|k}}}}{r^{{{lm}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.61615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.06631pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.9045pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.66557pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.86073pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.18623pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|k}}}}\partial_{l}X^{A}\partial_{m}X^{B}+r^{lm}\left(I\!I_{kl}\nu^{A}\partial_{m}X^{B}+I\!I_{km}\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}\right)\right)\,\text{,}

which are adding up to

\displaystyle\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{r}

\displaystyle=\Delta\boldsymbol{r}-\left(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\boldsymbol{r}+\boldsymbol{r}\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\right)+\left(2(\nabla\boldsymbol{r}):\boldsymbol{I\!I}+\boldsymbol{r}\nabla\mathcal{H}\right)\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\left(2(\nabla\boldsymbol{r}^{T}):\boldsymbol{I\!I}+(\nabla\mathcal{H})\boldsymbol{r}\right)+2(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}:\boldsymbol{r})\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{,}

where we use that
I\!I^{j}_{i|j}=I\!I^{j}_{j|i}=\mathcal{H}_{|i}
is valid, since
\boldsymbol{I\!I}
is curl-free.
Mixed proxy components
\mathchoice{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}=\partial_{k}(\eta_{L}^{A}\nu^{B})
yield

\displaystyle\partial_{k}(\eta_{L}^{A}\nu^{B})

\displaystyle=\partial_{k}(\eta_{L}^{i}\partial_{i}X^{A}\nu^{B})=\eta_{L|k}^{i}\partial_{i}X^{A}\nu^{B}+\eta_{L}^{i}I\!I_{ki}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}-\eta_{L}^{i}I\!I^{j}_{k}\partial_{i}X^{A}\partial_{j}X^{B}\,\text{.}

Substituting this into
\left[\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})\right]^{AB}
, the product rule gives the summands

\displaystyle g^{ij}\partial_{j}\left(\eta_{L|i}^{k}\partial_{k}X^{A}\nu^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\eta_{L\ |j}^{k|j}+g^{ij}\Gamma_{ij}^{l}\eta_{L|l}^{k}\right)\partial_{k}X^{A}\nu^{B}+\eta_{L|i}^{k}I\!I^{i}_{k}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}-\eta_{L|i}^{k}I\!I^{il}\partial_{k}X^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}

\displaystyle g^{ij}\partial_{j}\left(\eta_{L}^{k}I\!I_{ik}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=\eta_{L|j}^{k}I\!I^{j}_{k}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}+\eta_{L}^{k}\left(I\!I^{j}_{k|j}+g^{ij}\Gamma_{ji}^{l}I\!I_{lk}\right)\nu^{A}\nu^{B}-\eta_{L}^{k}I\!I^{j}_{k}I\!I^{l}_{j}\left(\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}-\nu^{A}\partial_{l}X\right)

\displaystyle-g^{ij}\partial_{j}\left(\eta_{L}^{k}I\!I^{l}_{i}\partial_{k}X^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=-\eta_{L|j}^{k}I\!I^{jl}\partial_{k}X^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}-\eta_{L}^{k}\left(\mathchoice{I\!I^{{{lj}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{I\!I^{{{lj}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{I\!I^{{{lj}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{I\!I^{{{lj}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}+g^{ij}\Gamma_{ji}^{m}I\!I^{l}_{m}\right)\partial_{k}X^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}-\eta_{L}^{k}I\!I^{l}_{i}\left(I\!I^{i}_{k}\nu^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}+I\!I^{i}_{l}\partial_{k}X^{A}\nu^{B}\right)

\displaystyle-g^{ij}\Gamma_{ij}^{k}\partial_{k}(\eta_{L}^{A}\nu^{B})

\displaystyle=-g^{ij}\Gamma_{ij}^{k}\left(\eta_{L|k}^{l}\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}+\eta_{L}^{l}I\!I_{kl}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}-\eta_{L}^{l}I\!I^{m}_{k}\partial_{l}X^{A}\partial_{m}X^{B}\right)\,\text{,}

which are adding up to

\displaystyle\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})

\displaystyle=-2(\nabla\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L})\boldsymbol{I\!I}-\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\otimes\nabla\mathcal{H}+\left(\Delta\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}-\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}-\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\right)\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}

\displaystyle\quad-2\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}+\left(2(\nabla\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}):\boldsymbol{I\!I}+\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}\nabla\mathcal{H}\right)\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{.}

Since
\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}
is compatible with transposition,
i. e. it is
\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R})=(\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}(\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}))^{T}
valid, this leads to

\displaystyle\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R})

\displaystyle=-2\boldsymbol{I\!I}(\nabla\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R})^{T}-(\nabla\mathcal{H})\otimes\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\left(\Delta\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}-\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}-\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-2\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\left(2(\nabla\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}):\boldsymbol{I\!I}+\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}\nabla\mathcal{H}\right)\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{.}

Mixed proxy components
\mathchoice{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}(\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}(\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}(\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{[\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}(\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})]^{{{AB}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.39691pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.63753pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.45537pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}=\partial_{k}(\phi\nu^{A}\nu^{B})
yield

\displaystyle\partial_{k}(\phi\nu^{A}\nu^{B})

\displaystyle=\phi_{|k}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}-\phi I\!I^{l}_{k}\left(\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}+\nu^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}\right)

Substituting this into
\left[\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}(\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})\right]^{AB}
, the product rule gives the summands

\displaystyle g^{ij}\partial_{j}\left(\phi_{|i}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\mathchoice{\phi^{{{|j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{\phi^{{{|j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{\phi^{{{|j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{\phi^{{{|j}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}+g^{ij}\Gamma_{ij}^{k}\phi_{|k}\right)\nu^{A}\nu^{B}-\phi_{|i}I\!I^{il}\left(\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}+\nu^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}\right)

\displaystyle-g^{ij}\partial_{j}\left(\phi I\!I^{l}_{i}\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=-\phi_{|j}I\!I^{jl}\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}-\phi\left(\mathchoice{I\!I^{{{lj}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{I\!I^{{{lj}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{I\!I^{{{lj}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{I\!I^{{{lj}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}+g^{ij}\Gamma_{ij}^{k}I\!I^{l}_{k}\right)\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}-\phi I\!I^{l}_{i}I\!I^{i}_{l}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}+\phi I\!I^{l}_{i}I\!I^{ik}\partial_{l}X^{A}\partial_{k}X^{B}

\displaystyle-g^{ij}\partial_{j}\left(\phi I\!I^{l}_{i}\nu^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}\right)

\displaystyle=-\phi_{|j}I\!I^{jl}\nu^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}-\phi\left(\mathchoice{I\!I^{{{jl}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{I\!I^{{{jl}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{I\!I^{{{jl}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}{I\!I^{{{jl}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.90955pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.65945pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.61388pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.28302pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.85681pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75485pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{|j}}}}+g^{ij}\Gamma_{ij}^{k}I\!I^{l}_{k}\right)\nu^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}-\phi I\!I^{l}_{i}I\!I^{i}_{l}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}+\phi I\!I^{l}_{i}I\!I^{ik}\partial_{k}X^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}

\displaystyle-g^{ij}\Gamma_{ij}^{k}\partial_{k}(\phi\nu^{A}\nu^{B})

\displaystyle=-g^{ij}\Gamma_{ij}^{k}\left(\phi_{|k}\nu^{A}\nu^{B}-\phi I\!I^{l}_{k}\left(\partial_{l}X^{A}\nu^{B}+\nu^{A}\partial_{l}X^{B}\right)\right)\,\text{,}

which are adding up to

\displaystyle\operatorname{\nabla}_{\mathrm{C}}(\phi\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu})

\displaystyle=2\phi\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}-\left(2\boldsymbol{I\!I}\nabla\phi+\phi\nabla\mathcal{H}\right)\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\left(2\boldsymbol{I\!I}\nabla\phi+\phi\nabla\mathcal{H}\right)+\left(\Delta\phi-2\phi\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})\right)\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\,\text{.}

∎

Lemma 27
.

For all symmetric tangential 2-tensor fields
\boldsymbol{s}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}:=\{\boldsymbol{r}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}\,|\,\boldsymbol{r}=\boldsymbol{r}^{T}\}
and tangential Q-tensor fields
\boldsymbol{q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
holds

\displaystyle\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{s}^{2}\boldsymbol{q})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{s}\right\|_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}^{2}\boldsymbol{q}\,\text{.}

Proof.

We use the Levi-Civita tensor
\boldsymbol{E}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{A}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{A}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{A}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{A}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}:=\{\boldsymbol{r}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}\,|\,\boldsymbol{r}=-\boldsymbol{r}^{T}\}
.
It is a skew-symmetric tangential 2-tensor field defined by its covariant proxy components
E_{ij}:=\sqrt{\det\boldsymbol{g}}\varepsilon_{ij}
,
where
\{\varepsilon_{ij}\}
are the Levi-Civita symbols, see
[
13
,
7
]
for more details.
This tensor field is very useful in many situations involving tangential tensor fields.
We use the properties that
\boldsymbol{r}\bot(\boldsymbol{r}\boldsymbol{E})
is valid for all
\boldsymbol{r}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
and
\boldsymbol{q}\boldsymbol{E}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
.
This yields

\displaystyle\left\langle\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{s}^{2}\boldsymbol{q}),\boldsymbol{q}\boldsymbol{E}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\boldsymbol{s}^{2}\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{q}\boldsymbol{E}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}=\left\langle\boldsymbol{s}\boldsymbol{q},(\boldsymbol{s}\boldsymbol{q})\boldsymbol{E}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}=0\,\text{.}

Since
\boldsymbol{q}^{2}=\frac{\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{q}^{2}}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}
is valid, see
[
13
, Cor A.4.]
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\langle\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{s}^{2}\boldsymbol{q}),\boldsymbol{q}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\boldsymbol{s}^{2}\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{q}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}=\left\langle\boldsymbol{s}^{2},\boldsymbol{q}^{2}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}=\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{s}^{2}\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{q}^{2}\,\text{.}

Assuming
\boldsymbol{q}\neq 0
everywhere without loss of generality, we can span the space of Q-tensor fields by
\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}=\operatorname{Span}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}\{\boldsymbol{q},\boldsymbol{q}\boldsymbol{E}\}
.
Due to this we get

\displaystyle\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{s}^{2}\boldsymbol{q})

\displaystyle=\frac{\left\langle\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{s}^{2}\boldsymbol{q}),\boldsymbol{q}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}}{\left\|\boldsymbol{q}\right\|_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}^{2}}\boldsymbol{q}+\frac{\left\langle\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{s}^{2}\boldsymbol{q}),\boldsymbol{q}\boldsymbol{E}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}}{\left\|\boldsymbol{q}\boldsymbol{E}\right\|_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}^{2}}\boldsymbol{q}\boldsymbol{E}=\frac{1}{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{s}\right\|_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}^{2}\boldsymbol{q}\,\text{,}

since
\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{s}^{2}=\left\|\boldsymbol{s}\right\|_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}^{2}
for all
\boldsymbol{s}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Sym}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
.
∎

Appendix B
Outsourced Calculations

B.1
Time Derivative on Scalar Fields

Local observer coordinate parameters
(y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2})
can be given by

\displaystyle y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{i}

\displaystyle=y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{i}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})=(\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}|_{t}^{-1}\circ\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}})(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})

depended on local material coordinate parameters
(y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})
at time
t
.
Therefore, with relation (
3
), a scalar field
f[\boldsymbol{X}_{\!\mathfrak{m}}]\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
and the pullback (
4
) yields

\displaystyle f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})

\displaystyle=f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))

\displaystyle(\Phi^{*_{0}}_{t,\tau}f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}]|_{t+\tau})(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})

\displaystyle=f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))\,\text{.}

Taylor expansion of the pullback at
\tau=0
gives

\displaystyle f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t+\tau,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))

\displaystyle=f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))+\tau\partial_{t}f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))

\displaystyle\quad+\tau\partial_{t}y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{i}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\partial_{i}f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))+\mathcal{O}(\tau^{2})\,\text{.}

(58)

To express
\partial_{t}y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{i}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})
also in terms of
y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{i}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})
we calculate

\displaystyle\partial_{t}y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{i}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})\partial_{i}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{dt}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))-\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})-\partial_{t}\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}[\boldsymbol{X}_{\!\mathfrak{m}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})-\boldsymbol{V}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}[\boldsymbol{X}_{\!\mathfrak{o}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{u}[\boldsymbol{X}_{\!\mathfrak{o}},\boldsymbol{X}_{\!\mathfrak{m}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))\,\text{,}

i. e. it holds

\partial_{t}y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{i}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})=u^{i}[\boldsymbol{X}_{\!\mathfrak{o}},\boldsymbol{X}_{\!\mathfrak{m}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))

w. r. t. the observer frame induced by
\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}
.
With
\dot{f}:=\operatorname{D}_{t}|_{\Phi^{*}_{t,\tau}=\Phi^{*_{0}}_{t,\tau}}f
and Taylor expansion (
58
),
the time derivative (
2
) becomes

\displaystyle\dot{f}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})

\displaystyle=\dot{f}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))

\displaystyle\quad+u^{i}[\boldsymbol{X}_{\!\mathfrak{o}},\boldsymbol{X}_{\!\mathfrak{m}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))\partial_{i}f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}),y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2}(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2}))\,\text{.}

Since none of the terms need to use the local material coordinate parameters
(y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})
at time
t
anymore, we get

\displaystyle\dot{f}[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2})

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2})+\nabla_{\boldsymbol{u}[\boldsymbol{X}_{\!\mathfrak{o}},\boldsymbol{X}_{\!\mathfrak{m}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2})}f[\boldsymbol{X}_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}](t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2})

finally by local evaluations at events
(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{o}}}^{2})
instead of
(t,y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{1},y_{{\!\mathfrak{m}}}^{2})
.

B.2
Decomposition of the Surface conforming
\operatorname{L}^{\!2}
-Gradient Flow (
37
)

In this section we are assuming the orthogonal surface conforming decomposition
\boldsymbol{Q}=\boldsymbol{q}+\beta(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}})\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}
,
with uniquely given
\boldsymbol{q}\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
and
\beta\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{0}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
,
i. e. it is
\boldsymbol{Q}=\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Q}}\left[\boldsymbol{q},0,\beta\right]
valid according to Q-tensor decomposition (
29
).
The decomposition of the left-handed side of (
37
) is already clarified with
(
33
) for the surface conforming material derivative and (
34
) for the Jaumann derivative.
For the elastic part we use the decomposition of the Laplace operator in corollary
26
with
\boldsymbol{r}=\boldsymbol{q}-\frac{\beta}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}
,

\boldsymbol{\eta}_{L}=\boldsymbol{\eta}_{R}=0
and
\phi=\beta
.
Hence with surface conforming projection (
32
), this yields

\displaystyle\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}^{\operatorname{C}_{\mathcal{S}}}\boldsymbol{Q}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}}\operatorname{\Delta}_{\mathrm{C}}\boldsymbol{Q}=\Delta\boldsymbol{q}-\frac{\Delta\beta}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}-\left(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\boldsymbol{q}+\boldsymbol{q}\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\right)+3\beta\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}+\left(2\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}:\boldsymbol{q}+\Delta\beta-3\beta\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})\right)\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}

\displaystyle=\Delta\boldsymbol{q}-\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}\left(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\left(2\boldsymbol{q}-3\beta\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right)\right)+\left(\Delta\beta+\left\langle\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2},2\boldsymbol{q}-3\beta\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}\right)\left(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right)

\displaystyle=\Delta\boldsymbol{q}-\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})\boldsymbol{q}+3\beta\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})+\left(\Delta\beta+\left\langle\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2},2\boldsymbol{q}-3\beta\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right\rangle_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{T}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}\right)\left(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right)\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}\,\text{.}

The bottom line follows from
2\operatorname{\Pi}_{\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.40283pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.25pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2}\boldsymbol{q})=\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{I\!I}^{2})\boldsymbol{q}
(lemma
27
).
For the thermotropic part we first calculate

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Q}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\boldsymbol{q}+\beta(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}})\right)^{2}=\boldsymbol{q}^{2}-\beta\boldsymbol{q}+\beta^{2}\left(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}+\frac{1}{4}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right)\,\text{.}

Its trace and Q-tensor part is

\displaystyle\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{Q}^{2}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{q}^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\beta^{2}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Q}^{2}-\frac{\operatorname{Tr}(\boldsymbol{Q}^{2})}{3}\left(\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}\right)

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{q}^{2}-\beta\boldsymbol{q}-\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{4}+\frac{\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{q}^{2}}{3}\right)\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}+\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{2}-\frac{\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{q}^{2}}{3}\right)\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}

\displaystyle=-\beta\boldsymbol{q}+\left(\frac{\beta^{2}}{2}-\frac{\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{q}^{2}}{3}\right)\left(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}\right)\in\mathchoice{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{C_{\mathcal{S}}Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathbb{R}^{3}|_{\mathcal{S}}\,\text{,}

where we use that
\boldsymbol{Id}=\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}+\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}
and
\boldsymbol{q}^{2}=\frac{\operatorname{Tr}\boldsymbol{q}^{2}}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}}

[
13
, Cor A.4.]
hold.
Eventually, orthogonality
(\boldsymbol{\nu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{Id}_{\mathcal{S}})\bot\mathchoice{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\operatorname{Q}\!^{{{2}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathcal{S}
results in the
decomposed surface conforming
\operatorname{L}^{\!2}
-gradient flow (
40
).
